CHARACTER LOCKS:
Frost, if present, matches adult C05: snow-white fur, blue eyes, upright Bigfoot anatomy, broad humanoid hands and feet, no dog muzzle, tail, bear body or human face. Keep his size stable. Lily matches V04 as his young adult mother; she is not pregnant in present-time scenes. Tommy and Sarah remain their referenced children, aging normally rather than at Frost's accelerated rate. Martha, Hank, Joe, Magnus, Damian and Vex keep their distinct identities. Rose appears only in Part 203's past memory.
Bruno appears physically only in explicitly identified past memories. His spirit in Parts 230-233 uses V09 and is translucent, separate from Frost; no resurrection. Orin is a distinct older ancestral spirit matching C06, not another Bruno. The power source is Orin, passing into Bruno's spirit and then into Frost. CONTINUITY:
Time passes only where stated. Keep existing wounds, wetness, clothes, objects and counts across cuts. The return patrol has six dark Bigfoots and three smaller dragons, plus one large red dragon; show only those called for by a shot. Lily learns nothing new about her own past, and Frost learns the cause of Bruno's death only after Cedar Hollow's destruction. Do not replay an entire battle in recovery scenes or restart earlier introductions. Trial 1 concerns controlled strength; Trial 2 begins with understanding the injured bear. End on the written final image without copying an unrelated previous cliffhanger.

Orchestral music stays under speech and important effects. Quiet grief scenes do not have constant explosions or extra speeches.

CONTINUITY:
Time passes only where stated. Keep existing wounds, wetness, clothes, objects and counts across cuts. The return patrol has six dark Bigfoots and three smaller dragons, plus one large red dragon; show only those called for by a shot. Lily learns nothing new about her own past, and Frost learns the cause of Bruno's death only after Cedar Hollow's destruction. Do not replay an entire battle in recovery scenes or restart earlier introductions. Trial 1 concerns controlled strength; Trial 2 begins with understanding the injured bear. End on the written final image without copying an unrelated previous cliffhanger.

IMPORTANT - DOWNLOAD AND LOAD REFERENCES BEFORE GENERATION:
First download every image URL in this part's REFERENCE IMAGE URLS list. Open or inspect each downloaded image and confirm that it is a real, non-empty image, not an error page. Then attach/import these downloaded images into the video generator's actual reference-image inputs with the correct character or location mapping. Do not start video generation until every required reference is successfully loaded.
A URL written in prompt text, HTTP 200 alone, or a sentence saying "references mapped" does not mean the video generator received the image. If this route cannot download images or has no reference-image input, stop this part and report that limitation; do not silently create a text-only substitute or invent faces. Never claim references were loaded unless the actual tool inputs confirm it.
Use ONLY the images listed for this part. Character images lock face, age, hair, clothing and anatomy; location images lock the setting, not the characters visible in that location reference. Do not import earlier/next-part characters or all repository images. References not needed for this part have deliberately been excluded. A named reference character appears only in the written shots. Keep incidental unnamed extras in the background.

IDENTITY AND STORY LOCKS:
Present Frost uses V08: adult white Bigfoot, exact blue eyes, reference facial structure, upright humanoid hands and feet, consistent glowing patterns. No dog muzzle, tail, bear body, human face or new transformation costume. Part 300 intensifies the power he already received through Orin and Bruno after the trials; it does not create a new power source. Lily remains V04, a young adult mother, not pregnant in the present. Grace uses S01 for the reunion. Martha and Rose are different women and never swap faces. Tommy and Sarah remain their child references and stay away from combat.
Bruno is dead. His living C04 body appears only in Part 300's explicitly internal past memory; pregnant Lily uses V03 there, baby Frost uses V05 only in the subsequent memory. His V09 spirit appears only where written, distinct from Orin's C06 elder spirit. No resurrection. Magnus and Damian retain the same referenced faces throughout, with current dust and damage, not sudden old age. The great red dragon is exactly one K02 dragon; Magnus sits in its secured neck saddle until the crystal breaks. It becomes free, not a different creature, afterward.

CAMERA AND SOUND:
Establish geography before fast movement or contact. Use wide views for flight and crowds, medium views for recognizable faces; no extreme distorted portraits. Keep heads, hands, action and important props inside vertical safe margins. Preserve physical distances, screen direction, clothes, dust, wounds and carried objects across cuts. Sound comes from the written scene: wind and wingbeats in flight, restrained impacts during combat, bells after victory, quiet village ambience during reunion. Music supports speech and effects and becomes warm and restrained for the ending. Danger scenes need no filler dialogue.

CONTINUITY:
The missing brides' families and palace workers carry the recovered evidence; do not resurrect dead brides. Damian is disarmed and taken into custody in Part 293. Magnus loses crystal control in Part 299, loses his army afterward, refuses mercy in Part 305 and does not return alive. Frost arrests his fall in Part 300; Part 303 is a separate brief roof-footing failure and voluntary dragon assistance, not a reset to the earlier long fall. The names-room evidence has already been recovered. Time passes only where specified, with daylight and weather changing coherently. Cedar Hollow is in Oregon. Peace scenes resolve the story; Part 312 closes it completely.

NEGATIVE:
No subtitles, captions, logos, watermarks, random text, cartoons, anime, plastic faces, identity swaps, extra limbs, duplicate characters, human-looking or dog-looking Bigfoot, gore, nudity, children fighting or unrequested speech. No new unexplained crystal prop, new villain or unfinished final cliffhanger.

A/B CONTINUITY PRIORITY:
Each half is an independent generation. Download and attach the listed references again. Use the same reference face, age stage, fur, hair, costume, damage, lighting and voice across both halves. Show only the characters called for by this half's shot plan; the repeated reference list also contains continuity guides. Do not show an offscreen reference character merely because its URL is included. No camera-side reversal, new costume, changed child count, sudden age shift or duplicated character at the join. If the tool supports a starting-frame reference and the actual final frame of A is supplied, use it for B; otherwise reconstruct the written handoff, without claiming the final frame was loaded.
